📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • CDC 协议正确识别了 300 个样本中的 291 个,产生 141 个真阳性、150 个真阴性、9 个假阴性,没有假阳性。 [10.1128/spectrum.01431-26]。
  • 在 53 例 猴痘 病例和 248 例匹配对照中,13.7% 的病例患有严重的 猴痘。CYV 斜率增加与调整后联合建模中获得 猴痘 的较高风险相关[风险比 1.11 (95% CI 1.04–1.18)]。
  • 主要发现包括确定了一个“沉默时代”(1970-2016),其特点是西非野生动物(主要是啮齿动物和小型哺乳动物)中 MPXV 的神秘地方性传播。2017 年尼日利亚的重新出现标志着一种范式转变,猴痘 从零星的农村儿科感染转变为持续的城市 传播,特别是在年轻成年男性中。基因组分析表明,进化枝 II 在大约 3500 年前与进化枝 I 分化,其中进化枝 IIb 表现出与 APOBEC3 介导的超突变一致的加速微进化,这表明早在 2014 年,人与人之间的 传播 链中就存在神秘循环。
  • 在无控制场景模拟中,第100天累计感染人数达到6331人。在从单控制到四重控制的15种最佳控制方案中,实施任何方案都可以有效减少感染总数。 [10.1186/s12889-026-28925-6]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The CDC protocol correctly identified 291 of 300 samples, yielding 141 true positives, 150 true negatives, 9 false negatives, and no false positives. [10.1128/spectrum.01431-26].
  • The mean age was 32.0 years (SD 10.7); 51.9% (2,056/3,960) were female, 80.2% resided in high-burden districts, 60.9% perceived themselves at risk, 18.7% had comprehensive knowledge of Mpox, 53.8% reported adequate prevention practice despite 93.2% message exposure, and comprehensive knowledge (aPR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15–1.52) and message exposure (aPR 1.88, 95% CI 1.31–2.71) were independently associated with practice.
  • Among 53 mpox cases and 248 matched controls, 13.7% of cases had severe mpox.
  • Key findings include the identification of an 'Era of Silence' (1970–2016) marked by cryptic enzootic circulation of MPXV in West African wildlife, primarily rodents and small mammals. [10.3390/zoonoticdis6030035].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • CDC 报告正在进行的 I 型分支和 II 型分支 猴痘 暴发 已确诊病例、与旅行相关的传播和低死亡率;指南强调 疫苗接种 和 监测。 [news-104d37adc31ac019]。
  • 自 2023 年底以来,中非和东非已报道了 I 支猴痘的 暴发,西欧和美国也报告了较新的 暴发。疾病预防控制中心已确认这些 暴发 并正在监测其传播情况。暴发 的特征是通过性接触、家庭接触和接触受感染的动物来表现 传播。
  • 纽约市健康和心理卫生局报告,从欧洲返回的旅行者中确诊的 猴痘 分支 I 病例有所增加;纽约市首例已知的 I 型分支病例于 2026 年 3 月报告(N2、N3、N4、N6)。 [news-5fa9b308f92ed364]。
  • 据报告,欧洲 猴痘 病例模式发生转变:分支 I 病例基本保持稳定,而分支 II 病例继续下降;ECDC 监测 更新于 2026 年 8 月 14 日发布。 [news-56942c994b437ddf]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • CDC reports ongoing clade I and clade II mpox outbreaks with confirmed cases, travel-associated spread, and low death rates; guidance emphasizes vaccination and surveillance. [news-104d37adc31ac019].
  • Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox have been reported in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with newer outbreaks in Western Europe and the U.S. The CDC has confirmed these outbreaks and is monitoring their spread.
  • NYC Department of Health and Mental Hygiene reports a rise in confirmed mpox clade I cases among travelers returning from Europe; first known clade I case in NYC was reported in March 2026 (N2, N3, N4, N6).
  • Reported shift in mpox case patterns in Europe: clade I cases remain broadly steady while clade II cases continue to decline; ECDC surveillance update released on 14 August 2026. [news-56942c994b437ddf].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Reviews in medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70196  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 5, e70196
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

猴痘 机载 传播:系统回顾。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.
作者： Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
摘要中文翻译自 2022 年 5 月以来,多个非流行地区均报告了 猴痘,并在不同的地理环境中同时发生了 暴发,引发了人们对更广泛的国际传播的担忧。然而,有关空气传播 传播 风险的证据仍然有限。我们进行了系统审查,以评估空气中 传播 的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒。检索了 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,包括环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和 队列研究 研究。数据提取包括研究特征(作者、年份、设计和背景)、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、随访、评估的呼吸道颗粒类型(飞沫和/或气溶胶)以及主要发现。在确定的 286 份记录中,有四项研究符合纳入标准。所有这些都是 2022 年至 2023 年间发表的环境研究。在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 到 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或飞沫中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。总体而言,现有证据有限,并不支持空气传播感染的明确风险。需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸途径的潜力。系统审查注册:PROSPERO:CRD42023391123。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 猴痘 空气传播的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒,因为 猴痘 已在多个非流行地区报告,并担心国际传播。
设计与对象
对猴痘的空气传播传播的风险进行了系统评价,包括2022年至2023年期间发表的环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和队列研究研究。
核心方法
该综述涉及搜索 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,提取有关研究特征、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、评估的呼吸颗粒物和主要发现的数据。
主要结果
在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 至 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或液滴中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,现有证据并不支持空气传播感染的明显风险,强调需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸道途径的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,目前的证据不足以证实猴痘在空气中传播,这对公共卫生措施以及制定有针对性的监测和预防策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
证据有限且基于环境研究,可能无法完全代表人类 传播 动态,导致结论的置信度评估适中。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.

Authors: Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
Original abstract (English)
Since May 2022, mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions, with concurrent outbreaks in diverse geographic settings, raising concerns about broader international spread. However, evidence regarding the risk of airborne transmission remains limited. We conducted a systematic review to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission and the presence of viable viruses in air samples. PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched for studies published in English, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies. Data extraction included study characteristics (authors, year, design, and setting), sample size, diagnostic methods, participant characteristics, follow-up, type of respiratory particles assessed (droplets and/or aerosols), and key findings. Of 286 records identified, four studies met the inclusion criteria. All were environmental studies published between 2022 and 2023. MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission. Overall, the available evidence is limited and does not support a clear risk of airborne infection. Further well-designed studies in human settings are needed to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes. SYSTEMATIC REVIEW REGISTRATION: PROSPERO: CRD42023391123.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission of mpox and the presence of viable viruses in air samples, as mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions with concerns about international spread.
Design and population
A systematic review was conducted to assess the risk of airborne transmission of mpox, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies published between 2022 and 2023.
Core methods
The review involved searching PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library for studies published in English, extracting data on study characteristics, sample size, diagnostic methods, participant features, respiratory particles assessed, and key findings.
Main results
MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the available evidence does not support a clear risk of airborne infection, highlighting the need for further well-designed studies in human settings to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that current evidence is insufficient to confirm airborne transmission of mpox, which has implications for public health measures and the development of targeted surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited and based on environmental studies, which may not fully represent human transmission dynamics, leading to a moderate confidence assessment in the conclusions.

乌干达 猴痘 传播 风险的社会生态决定因素:绘制社区层面的风险和保护因素,一项多区横断面研究

Socio-ecological determinants of Mpox transmission risk in Uganda: mapping community-level risk and protective factors, a multi-district cross-sectional study
作者： ingrid ampeire, Richard Kabanda, Misaki Wayengera, Takudzwa Marembo, Cathy Asiimwe Tabaro, Mosoka Papa Fallah, Henry Kyobe Bosa, ampeire, i., Tabaro, C. A., Fallah, M. P.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 2024 年以来,乌干达面临着 Ib 猴痘 进化枝的持续社区 传播,迫切需要了解风险和保护因素如何在社会生态中分布,以便为预防策略提供信息。
设计与对象
2025 年 2 月至 3 月,采用多阶段抽样的方式,对乌干达高负担区和低负担区的 3,960 名 15 岁至 65 岁的社区成员进行了一项多区、以社区为基础的横断面调查。
核心方法
使用平板电脑上管理的结构化工具对参与者进行采访,并对个人、家庭、社区和结构层面的特征进行了描述性总结;具有区域聚类稳健标准误差的改进泊松回归模型估计了粗略和调整后的 患病率 比率,以实现充分的 猴痘 预防和控制实践。
主要结果
The mean age was 32.0 years (SD 10.7); 51.9% (2,056/3,960) were female, 80.2% resided in high-burden districts, 60.9% perceived themselves at risk, 18.7% had comprehensive knowledge of Mpox, 53.8% reported adequate prevention practice despite 93.2% message exposure, and comprehensive knowledge (aPR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15–1.52) and message exposure (aPR 1.88, 95% CI 1.31–2.71) were independently associated with practice.
解释与创新
该研究解释说,猴痘 传播 风险集中在高负担地区,并与沉重的合并症负担重叠,但保护实践主要是由可改变的认知因素(知识和信息暴露)驱动的,而不是生物学特征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,社区层面的干预措施将几乎普遍的信息转化为准确、可操作的知识,可能会对乌干达的 Ib 猴痘 分支产生最大的保护效果。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推理,对自我报告的实践和知识的依赖可能会引入社会期望偏差;尽管样本很大且具有代表性,但由于这些局限性,证据被评估为中等。

Socio-ecological determinants of Mpox transmission risk in Uganda: mapping community-level risk and protective factors, a multi-district cross-sectional study

Authors: ingrid ampeire, Richard Kabanda, Misaki Wayengera, Takudzwa Marembo, Cathy Asiimwe Tabaro, Mosoka Papa Fallah, Henry Kyobe Bosa, ampeire, i., Tabaro, C. A., Fallah, M. P.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Uganda is among the African countries most affected by the ongoing clade Ib Mpox (monkeypox) outbreak, with sustained community transmission since 2024. Effective community-level prevention depends on understanding how risk and protective factors are distributed across the social ecology. We applied a socio-ecological framework to map the determinants of Mpox transmission risk and protective practice in affected districts. Methods We conducted a multi-district, community-based cross-sectional survey of 3,960 community members aged 15–65 years across high- and low-burden districts in Uganda between February and March 2025. Participants were selected by multistage sampling and interviewed using a structured tool administered on tablets. Individual-, household-, community- and structural-level characteristics were summarised descriptively. A modified Poisson regression model with district-clustered robust standard errors was used to estimate crude and adjusted prevalence ratios (cPR, aPR) for adequate Mpox prevention and control practice; the multivariable model included 3,916 participants with complete covariate data. Analyses were performed in R version 4.5.0. Results The mean age was 32.0 years (SD 10.7); 51.9% (2,056/3,960) were female and most had secondary education (54.2%). Comorbidity burden was substantial (sexually transmitted infections 28.3%, HIV 13.9%, tuberculosis 8.2%, malnutrition 6.2%). Four in five participants (80.2%) resided in high-burden districts and 60.9% perceived themselves at risk, yet only 18.7% had comprehensive knowledge of Mpox and 53.8% reported adequate prevention practice despite near-universal message exposure (93.2%). In adjusted analysis, comprehensive knowledge (aPR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15–1.52) and message exposure (aPR 1.88, 95% CI 1.31–2.71) were by far the strongest protective determinants; several occupational, income, education and religious categories were also independently associated with practice, whereas biological comorbidities were not. Conclusions Mpox transmission risk in Uganda is concentrated in high-burden districts and overlaps with a heavy comorbidity burden. Although some social and structural factors were independently associated with protective practice, it was driven primarily by the modifiable cognitive factors of knowledge and message exposure rather than by biological characteristics. Community-level interventions that convert near-universal message reach into accurate, actionable knowledge are likely to yield the greatest protective gains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Uganda faces sustained community transmission of clade Ib Mpox since 2024, creating an urgent need to understand how risk and protective factors are distributed across the social ecology to inform prevention strategies.
Design and population
A multi-district, community-based cross-sectional survey was conducted among 3,960 community members aged 15–65 years across high- and low-burden districts in Uganda between February and March 2025 using multistage sampling.
Core methods
Participants were interviewed using a structured tool administered on tablets, and individual-, household-, community- and structural-level characteristics were summarised descriptively; a modified Poisson regression model with district-clustered robust standard errors estimated crude and adjusted prevalence ratios for adequate Mpox prevention and control practice.
Main results
The mean age was 32.0 years (SD 10.7); 51.9% (2,056/3,960) were female, 80.2% resided in high-burden districts, 60.9% perceived themselves at risk, 18.7% had comprehensive knowledge of Mpox, 53.8% reported adequate prevention practice despite 93.2% message exposure, and comprehensive knowledge (aPR 1.32, 95% CI 1.15–1.52) and message exposure (aPR 1.88, 95% CI 1.31–2.71) were independently associated with practice.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets that Mpox transmission risk is concentrated in high-burden districts and overlaps with a heavy comorbidity burden, but protective practice is driven primarily by modifiable cognitive factors—knowledge and message exposure—rather than biological characteristics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings imply that community-level interventions converting near-universal message reach into accurate, actionable knowledge are likely to yield the greatest protective gains against clade Ib Mpox in Uganda.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on self-reported practice and knowledge may introduce social desirability bias; the evidence is assessed as moderate due to these limitations despite a large, representative sample.

世界卫生组织与疾病控制和预防中心 猴痘病毒 分子检测实时 PCR 检测的诊断性能比较

Comparison of diagnostic performance between the World Health Organization and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention real-time PCR assays for molecular detection of the mpox virus
作者： Darwin Paredes-Núñez, Silvia Salgado, Martha Sánchez, José Echevarría, Johanna Parrales, Andrés Tinizaray, Andrea Belén Salcedo Maldonado, Solón Alberto Orlando, Miguel Angel Garcia Bereguiain, Miguel Ángel García-Bereguiain
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 (猴痘) 在全球的出现和传播凸显了对准确可靠的分子诊断工具的需求。实时PCR仍然是检测猴痘病毒 (MPXV)的参考方法;然而,可用协议之间的差异可能会影响诊断性能。本研究的目的是使用厄瓜多尔的临床样本,比较美国疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC) 实时 PCR 检测与世界卫生组织 (WHO) 参考分子方案的诊断准确性。对疑似 猴痘 病例的 300 份标本进行回顾性分析,包括病灶拭子、水泡样本和血清,其中 150 份先前经 WHO 检测确认为 MPXV 阳性,150 份为 MPXV 阴性。使用标准化方案提取 DNA,并使用 CDC 检测对所有样本进行测试。CDC 协议正确识别了 300 个样本中的 291 个,产生 141 个真阳性、150 个真阴性、9 个假阴性,没有假阳性。总体一致性为 97% (95% CI: 94–98),敏感性为 94% (95% CI: 89–97),特异性为 100% (95% CI: 98–100)。阳性预测值为100%,阴性预测值为94%(95% CI:90-97)。获得的科恩卡帕系数为 0.94(95% CI:0.90-0.98)。这些研究结果表明,与 WHO 参考方案相比,CDC 实时 PCR 检测为 MPXV 检测提供了高诊断准确性和出色的特异性,支持将 CDC 和 WHO 实时 PCR 检测用作临床和 监测 设置中的可靠诊断工具。重要性 MPXV 在全球的出现和传播凸显了对准确 PCR 诊断工具的需求。这是第一项对美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 实时 PCR 检测和世界卫生组织 (WHO) 的 MPXV PCR 方案进行比较的研究,这两个机构是全球公认的传染病参考文献 诊断。我们表明,两种 PCR 方案对于 MPXV 诊断 都非常可靠,并且可以在全球任何实验室中实施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 (猴痘) 在全球的出现和传播凸显了对准确可靠的分子诊断工具的需求。
设计与对象
对疑似 猴痘 病例的 300 份标本进行回顾性分析,包括病灶拭子、水泡样本和血清,其中 150 份先前经 WHO 检测确认为 MPXV 阳性,150 份为 MPXV 阴性。
核心方法
使用标准化方案提取 DNA,并使用 CDC 检测对所有样本进行测试。
主要结果
CDC 协议正确识别了 300 个样本中的 291 个,产生 141 个真阳性、150 个真阴性、9 个假阴性,没有假阳性。
解释与创新
这些结果表明,与 WHO 参考方案相比,CDC 实时 PCR 检测为 MPXV 检测提供了高诊断准确性和出色的特异性。
科研与公卫意义
结果支持在临床和 监测 环境中使用 CDC 和 WHO 实时 PCR 检测作为可靠的诊断工具。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Comparison of diagnostic performance between the World Health Organization and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention real-time PCR assays for molecular detection of the mpox virus

Authors: Darwin Paredes-Núñez, Silvia Salgado, Martha Sánchez, José Echevarría, Johanna Parrales, Andrés Tinizaray, Andrea Belén Salcedo Maldonado, Solón Alberto Orlando, Miguel Angel Garcia Bereguiain, Miguel Ángel García-Bereguiain
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The global emergence and spread of monkeypox (mpox) have underscored the need for accurate and reliable molecular diagnostic tools. Real-time PCR remains the reference method for detecting mpox virus (MPXV); however, variations among available protocols may affect diagnostic performance. The objective of this study was to compare the diagnostic accuracy of the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) of the USA real-time PCR assay with the World Health Organization (WHO) reference molecular protocol, using clinical samples from Ecuador. A retrospective analysis was conducted on 300 specimens from suspected mpox cases, including lesion swabs, vesicular samples, and serum, of which 150 were previously confirmed as MPXV-positive and 150 as MPXV-negative by the WHO assay. DNA was extracted using a standardized protocol, and all samples were tested with the CDC assay. The CDC protocol correctly identified 291 of 300 samples, yielding 141 true positives, 150 true negatives, 9 false negatives, and no false positives. The overall agreement was 97% (95% CI: 94–98), with a sensitivity of 94% (95% CI: 89–97) and a specificity of 100% (95% CI: 98–100). The positive predictive value was 100%, and the negative predictive value was 94% (95% CI: 90–97). The Cohen’s kappa coefficient obtained was 0.94 (95% CI: 0.90-0.98). These findings demonstrate that the CDC real-time PCR assay provides high diagnostic accuracy and excellent specificity for MPXV detection compared with the WHO reference protocol, supporting the use of both CDC and WHO real-time PCR assays as reliable diagnostic tools in clinical and surveillance settings. IMPORTANCE The global emergence and spread of MPXV have underscored the need for accurate PCR diagnostic tools. This is the first study to address a comparison between the PCR protocols for MPXV from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) of the USA real-time PCR assay and the World Health Organization (WHO), two institutions globally acknowledged as references for infectious disease diagnosis. We show that both PCR protocols are very reliable for MPXV diagnosis and could be implemented for any laboratory worldwide indistinctly.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The global emergence and spread of monkeypox (mpox) have underscored the need for accurate and reliable molecular diagnostic tools.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 300 specimens from suspected mpox cases, including lesion swabs, vesicular samples, and serum, of which 150 were previously confirmed as MPXV-positive and 150 as MPXV-negative by the WHO assay.
Core methods
DNA was extracted using a standardized protocol, and all samples were tested with the CDC assay.
Main results
The CDC protocol correctly identified 291 of 300 samples, yielding 141 true positives, 150 true negatives, 9 false negatives, and no false positives.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate that the CDC real-time PCR assay provides high diagnostic accuracy and excellent specificity for MPXV detection compared with the WHO reference protocol.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the use of both CDC and WHO real-time PCR assays as reliable diagnostic tools in clinical and surveillance settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

猴痘 感染和 疫苗 诱导的免疫之间的血清学差异:小型综述

Serological differentiation between Mpox infection and vaccine-induced immunity: a mini-review
作者： Zhumagali Koshemetov, Yerbol Bulatov, Orazbek Serikbayov, Kuandyk Zhugunissov
摘要中文翻译近年来,猴痘 已成为全球重大公共卫生威胁。该病毒的广泛分布,加上历史天花 疫苗接种 的遗留问题,强调了从血清学角度区分自然 猴痘 感染和 疫苗 诱导的体液 免疫应答 的迫切需要。然而,正痘病毒 物种之间的高度抗原相似性导致交叉反应抗体的产生,限制了常规血清学测定的分化能力。这篇小型综述评估了当前旨在区分 猴痘 感染和 疫苗 诱导免疫的血清学方法。具体来说,我们分析多重免疫测定、基于直系同源抗原的比较比率、机器学习支持的血清学模型、基于肽的平台和基于重组蛋白的酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)方法。本综述的结果表明,多重免疫测定、基于直系同源抗原比率的方法和机器学习算法在区分自然 猴痘 感染和 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 方面表现出最高的区分潜力。此外,MPXV A27L 和 ATI-N-CPXV 抗原可能被认为是血清学分化的有希望的候选者,尽管它们的性能需要进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了区分 猴痘 感染和 疫苗 诱导的免疫的公共卫生需求,疫苗 物种之间的交叉反应抗体使这种区别变得复杂。
设计与对象
该论文是一篇小型综述,综合了现有的血清学方法,而不是收集新的原始数据。
核心方法
该综述评估了用于血清学分化的多重免疫测定、直系同源抗原比率、机器学习模型、基于肽的平台和重组蛋白 ELISA 方法。
主要结果
该综述发现,多重免疫测定、基于直系同源抗原比率的方法和机器学习算法显示出区分自然 猴痘 感染和 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的最高区分潜力。
解释与创新
作者强调 MPXV A27L 和 ATI-N-CPXV 抗原是血清学分化的有希望的候选者,并指出它们的性能需要进一步研究。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为血清学检测开发和公共卫生策略提供了信息,以区分 猴痘、监测 和 诊断 中感染引起的免疫和 疫苗 引起的免疫。
局限与证据强度
由于这是一篇没有主要数据的叙述性综述,证据强度受到选择和出版偏见的限制;需要进一步的实验验证。

Serological differentiation between Mpox infection and vaccine-induced immunity: a mini-review

Authors: Zhumagali Koshemetov, Yerbol Bulatov, Orazbek Serikbayov, Kuandyk Zhugunissov
Original abstract (English)
In recent years, mpox has emerged as a significant global public health threat. The widespread distribution of the virus, coupled with the legacy of historical smallpox vaccination, has underscored the critical need to serologically distinguish between natural mpox infection and vaccine-induced humoral immune responses. However, the high antigenic similarity among Orthopoxvirus species leads to the production of cross-reactive antibodies, limiting the differentiation capacity of conventional serological assays. This mini-review evaluates current serological approaches aimed at discriminating between mpox infection and vaccine-induced immunity. Specifically, we analyze multiplexed immunoassays, comparative ratios based on orthologous antigens, machine learning-supported serological models, peptide-based platforms, and recombinant protein-based enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) methods. The findings of this review indicate that multiplex immunoassays, orthologous antigen ratio-based approaches, and machine learning algorithms exhibit the highest discriminatory potential for distinguishing natural mpox infection from vaccine-induced immune responses. In addition, MPXV A27L and ATI-N-CPXV antigens may be considered promising candidates for serological differentiation, although their performance requires further investigation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the public-health need to distinguish mpox infection from vaccine-induced immunity, a distinction complicated by cross-reactive antibodies among Orthopoxvirus species.
Design and population
The paper is a mini-review that synthesizes existing serological approaches rather than collecting new primary data.
Core methods
The review evaluates multiplexed immunoassays, orthologous antigen ratios, machine learning models, peptide-based platforms, and recombinant protein ELISA methods for serological differentiation.
Main results
The review finds that multiplex immunoassays, orthologous antigen ratio-based approaches, and machine learning algorithms show the highest discriminatory potential for distinguishing natural mpox infection from vaccine-induced immune responses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight MPXV A27L and ATI-N-CPXV antigens as promising candidates for serological differentiation, noting that their performance requires further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform serological assay development and public-health strategies for differentiating infection-induced from vaccine-induced immunity in mpox surveillance and diagnosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
Because this is a narrative review without primary data, evidence strength is limited by selection and publication bias; further experimental validation is needed.

HIV 感染者中复制年病毒血症的纵向趋势与 猴痘 获得相关,但与 猴痘 严重程度无关

Longitudinal trends in copy-years viremia associated with mpox acquisition but not mpox severity in people with HIV
作者： Lauren F. O’Connor, Anne K. Monroe, Yun Ji, Anuja Baskaran, Amanda D. Castel, Angelo F. Elmi, Lauren O'Connor, DC Cohort Executive Committee
摘要中文翻译目的:高病毒载量和低 CD4 + 细胞计数可能与 猴痘 获得风险增加相关。然而,先前的研究尚未评估 HIV 实验室测量的纵向趋势(以随时间的斜率测量)是否与 猴痘 获得或严重 猴痘 相关。因此,我们评估了 CD4+ 细胞计数(细胞/μl)、病毒载量(拷贝数/ml)和拷贝年病毒血症(CYV)(拷贝数×年/ml)的纵向趋势是否与 猴痘 获得风险增加相关。设计:我们在 DC 队列研究 的参与者中进行了一项巢式病例对照研究,这是一项针对华盛顿特区 HIV 感染者 (PWH) 的纵向研究。方法:病例被定义为具有 猴痘 的参与者,并使用风险集抽样与五个对照进行匹配。使用两阶段建模和联合建模方法来评估 CD4 + 细胞计数、病毒载量和 CYV 的纵向趋势是否与 猴痘 获取相关。结果:我们评估了 53 例 猴痘 病例和 248 例匹配对照,其中 13.7% 的病例患有严重 猴痘。根据联合建模方法,在调整 AIDS 和已知的抗逆转录病毒治疗方案后,CYV 斜率增加与 猴痘 获得风险增加相关[风险比 (95% CI):1.11 (1.04–1.18)]。结论:在评估 猴痘 风险时,临床医生应考虑患者的整个病毒载量史,而不仅仅是某一时间点测量的病毒载量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 HIV 相关实验室测量的纵向趋势是否与 HIV 感染者(高危人群)的 猴痘 获得或严重程度相关。先前的工作尚未评估与 猴痘 结果相关的 CD4+ 细胞计数、病毒载量或拷贝年病毒血症 (CYV) 的基于斜率的趋势。
设计与对象
在 DC 队列研究 内进行了一项巢式病例对照研究,这是一项针对华盛顿特区 HIV 感染者的纵向 队列研究。使用风险集抽样将 猴痘 病例与五个对照进行匹配。
核心方法
使用两阶段联合建模方法来评估 CD4+ 细胞计数、病毒载量以及 CYV 和 猴痘 采集的纵向趋势之间的关联。通过调整艾滋病状况和抗逆转录病毒治疗方案,对病例和对照进行匹配和分析。
主要结果
在 53 例 猴痘 病例和 248 例匹配对照中,13.7% 的病例患有严重的 猴痘。CYV 斜率增加与调整后联合建模中获得 猴痘 的较高风险相关[风险比 1.11 (95% CI 1.04–1.18)]。
解释与创新
作者将 CYV 上升和 猴痘 获取之间的关联解释为随着时间的推移累积病毒载量而不是单点病毒载量可以更好地预测风险的证据。他们强调了在这种情况下使用基于纵向坡度的指标的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,临床医生应将纵向病毒载量历史纳入 HIV 感染者的 猴痘 风险评估中,从而有可能改善这一高危人群的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的嵌套病例对照设计和对现有 队列研究 数据的依赖限制了 DC 队列研究 之外的因果推断和普遍性。由于样本量和潜在的未测量的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Longitudinal trends in copy-years viremia associated with mpox acquisition but not mpox severity in people with HIV

Authors: Lauren F. O’Connor, Anne K. Monroe, Yun Ji, Anuja Baskaran, Amanda D. Castel, Angelo F. Elmi, Lauren O'Connor, DC Cohort Executive Committee
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: High viral load and low CD4 + cell count may be associated with increased risk of mpox acquisition. However, prior studies have not evaluated whether longitudinal trends in HIV lab measures, measured as the slope over time, are associated with mpox acquisition or severe mpox. Therefore, we evaluated whether longitudinal trends in CD4 + cell count (cells/μl), viral load (copies/ml), and copy-years viremia (CYV) (copies × years/ml) were associated with increased risk of mpox acquisition. Design: We conducted a nested case–control study among participants in the DC Cohort, a longitudinal study on people with HIV (PWH) in Washington, DC. Methods: Cases were defined as participants with mpox and were matched to five controls using risk set-sampling. Both two-stage modeling and joint modeling approaches were used to evaluate whether longitudinal trends in CD4 + cell count, viral load, and CYV were associated with mpox acquisition. Results: We evaluated 53 cases of mpox and 248 matched controls, with 13.7% of cases having severe mpox. An increased slope of CYV was associated with an increased risk of mpox acquisition based on the joint modeling approach, after adjusting for AIDS and known antiretroviral regimen [hazard ratio (95% CI): 1.11 (1.04–1.18)]. Conclusion: Clinicians should consider a patient's entire history of viral load, not just viral load measured at one point in time, when evaluating risk of mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether longitudinal trends in HIV-related laboratory measures are associated with mpox acquisition or severity among people with HIV, a population at elevated risk. Prior work had not evaluated slope-based trends in CD4+ cell count, viral load, or copy-years viremia (CYV) in relation to mpox outcomes.
Design and population
A nested case–control study was conducted within the DC Cohort, a longitudinal cohort of people with HIV in Washington, DC. Cases with mpox were matched to five controls using risk set sampling.
Core methods
Two-stage and joint modeling approaches were used to assess associations between longitudinal trends in CD4+ cell count, viral load, and CYV and mpox acquisition. Cases and controls were matched and analyzed with adjustment for AIDS status and antiretroviral regimen.
Main results
Among 53 mpox cases and 248 matched controls, 13.7% of cases had severe mpox. An increased slope of CYV was associated with higher risk of mpox acquisition in joint modeling after adjustment [hazard ratio 1.11 (95% CI 1.04–1.18)].
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the association between rising CYV and mpox acquisition as evidence that cumulative viral burden over time, rather than single-point viral load, better predicts risk. They highlighted the novelty of using longitudinal slope-based metrics in this context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that clinicians should incorporate longitudinal viral load histories into mpox risk assessment for people with HIV, potentially improving prevention strategies in this high-risk group.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s nested case–control design and reliance on existing cohort data limit causal inference and generalizability beyond the DC Cohort. Evidence strength is moderate due to sample size and potential unmeasured confounding.

猴痘 与动物 储存宿主 耦合的数学建模:稳定性、灵敏度以及对 2022 US 暴发 的校准

Mathematical Modeling of Mpox with Animal Reservoir Coupling: Stability, Sensitivity, and Calibration to the 2022 US Outbreak
作者： Mst. Srabony Akhter, Md. Ahnaf Sadik Inan, Ashrafi Meher Niger
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 是一种新出现的人畜共患疾病,由于其具有动物传人 跨物种溢出 和持续人传人 传播 的能力,引起了重大的公共卫生问题。本文开发了一个耦合的 SVEIQR-SEI 数学框架,捕获动物 储存宿主 和人类之间的 传播 动态,并结合 疫苗接种 和检疫干预措施。数学分析建立基本再生数(R0 = max{Rr,Rh}),证明当R0时无病平衡局部渐近稳定
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 由于其人畜共患 跨物种溢出 潜力和持续的人与人之间的 传播 造成公共卫生风险,因此需要了解动物与人之间的 传播 动态并评估干预措施。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一个耦合的 SVEIQR-SEI 数学框架来模拟动物 储存宿主 和人类之间的 传播,其中结合了 疫苗接种 和检疫干预措施。
核心方法
该分析建立了基本再生数 (R0 = max{Rr,Rh}),并使用数学建模技术证明了当 R0 < 1 时无病平衡的局部渐近稳定性。
主要结果
研究表明,当基本再生数R0小于1时,无病平衡是局部渐近稳定的。
解释与创新
作者将稳定性条件解释为表明将 R0 降低到 1 以下的干预措施可以防止持续的 传播,突出了 猴痘 控制策略的耦合建模的价值。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疫苗接种 和针对动物和人类隔间的检疫干预措施可以有效降低 传播 风险并为 暴发 做好准备提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于数学分析,没有经验验证,降低了普遍性,并给现实世界的适用性留下了不确定性。

Mathematical Modeling of Mpox with Animal Reservoir Coupling: Stability, Sensitivity, and Calibration to the 2022 US Outbreak

Authors: Mst. Srabony Akhter, Md. Ahnaf Sadik Inan, Ashrafi Meher Niger
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mpox, an emerging zoonotic disease, presents significant public health concerns due to its capacity for animal-to-human spillover and sustained human-to-human transmission. This paper develops a coupled SVEIQR-SEI mathematical framework capturing transmission dynamics between animal reservoirs and human populations, incorporating vaccination and quarantine interventions. Mathematical analysis establishes the basic reproduction number (R0 = max{Rr, Rh}), proving that the disease-free equilibrium is locally asymptotically stable when R0
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox poses public health risks due to its zoonotic spillover potential and sustained human-to-human transmission, creating a need to understand coupled animal-human transmission dynamics and evaluate interventions.
Design and population
The study develops a coupled SVEIQR-SEI mathematical framework to model transmission between animal reservoirs and human populations, incorporating vaccination and quarantine interventions.
Core methods
The analysis establishes the basic reproduction number (R0 = max{Rr, Rh}) and proves local asymptotic stability of the disease-free equilibrium when R0 < 1 using mathematical modeling techniques.
Main results
The study demonstrates that the disease-free equilibrium is locally asymptotically stable when the basic reproduction number R0 is less than one.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the stability condition as indicating that interventions reducing R0 below one can prevent sustained transmission, highlighting the value of coupled modeling for mpox control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that vaccination and quarantine interventions targeting both animal and human compartments can effectively reduce transmission risk and inform outbreak preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to mathematical analysis without empirical validation, reducing generalizability and leaving uncertainty about real-world applicability.

针对猴痘病毒 H3L 糖蛋白的下一代多表位亚基 疫苗 的结构引导设计,具有预测的先天和适应性免疫参与

Structure-Guided Design of a Next-Generation Multi-Epitope Subunit Vaccine Targeting the Monkeypox Virus H3L Glycoprotein with Predicted Innate and Adaptive Immune Engagement
作者： Divyesh P, Nagin Sree K, Ganeshkumar Y, Jasmin Padhan, Kumar K M, P D, K NS, Y G, M KK, Kumar Kumar M
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 代表了全球不断升级的公共卫生问题,因此需要开发独立于先前天花免疫史的定制 疫苗接种 策略。这项研究引入了一种基于机制的、基于多表位的亚基 疫苗 制剂,针对 H3L 表面暴露的糖蛋白,该制剂具有关键的生物学功能,包括病毒-宿主 细胞相互作用、核染色质重塑和 IL1A 依赖性炎症级联触发,从而导致多器官病理学。方法计算设计流程集成了多个互补的免疫信息学工具,即 IEDB、BepiPred-2.0、NetMHCpan EL 4.1、VaxiJen v2.0、AllerTOP 和 ToxinPred,以生成免疫功能、非过敏性、无毒 CTL、HTL 和 B 细胞决定簇的精选集合,预期覆盖约 > 90% 的全球 HLA 等位基因分布。通过 EAAAK 连接体连接的 N 端 β-防御素免疫佐剂被掺入以增强先天免疫激活。利用 RoseTTAFold 进行的计算 3D 结构预测证实了高质量的立体化学性质。结果通过 HDOCK 进行的分子对接证明了 疫苗 构建体与关键免疫识别分子(TLR2、TLR4-MD2、MHC-I、MHC-II)之间具有良好的、稳定的相互作用。随后使用 GROMACS 进行的 100 纳秒的分子动力学模拟揭示了所有 疫苗 受体配对的持续结构完整性和结合持久性。结论 该构建体被定位为临床前 MPXV 疫苗 评估的非常有前途的免疫原性候选者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决猴痘病毒引起的日益严重的全球公共卫生问题,重点是制定不依赖于先前天花免疫史的定制 疫苗接种 策略。
设计与对象
该研究涉及针对猴痘病毒 H3L 糖蛋白的多表位亚基 疫苗 的计算设计,该亚基在病毒 - 宿主 细胞相互作用、核染色质重塑和 IL1A 依赖性炎症级联触发中发挥关键作用。
核心方法
疫苗 设计利用集成了 IEDB、BepiPred-2.0、NetMHCpan EL 4.1、VaxiJen v2.0、AllerTOP 和 ToxinPred 等免疫信息学工具的计算管道来识别具有免疫功能、非过敏性和无毒的表位,覆盖超过 90% 的全球 HLA 等位基因分布。
主要结果
通过 HDOCK 的分子对接显示 疫苗 构建体和免疫识别分子(TLR2、TLR4-MD2、MHC-I、MHC-II)之间存在积极有利且稳定的相互作用。使用 GROMACS 进行超过 100 纳秒的分子动力学模拟证实了所有 疫苗 受体配对的持续结构完整性和结合持久性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 疫苗 构建体具有参与先天性和适应性 免疫应答 的潜力的证据,强调了其作为猴痘病毒的结构引导的多表位亚基 疫苗 的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种 疫苗 构建体可以为有效的 监测 和 猴痘 疾病的预防策略的开发提供信息,特别是在之前没有接种过天花免疫的人群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算模型,不包括实验验证,这将证据的强度降低到中等。

Structure-Guided Design of a Next-Generation Multi-Epitope Subunit Vaccine Targeting the Monkeypox Virus H3L Glycoprotein with Predicted Innate and Adaptive Immune Engagement

Authors: Divyesh P, Nagin Sree K, Ganeshkumar Y, Jasmin Padhan, Kumar K M, P D, K NS, Y G, M KK, Kumar Kumar M
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose The monkeypox virus (MPXV) represents an escalating public health concern globally, necessitating the development of tailored vaccination strategies that function independently of prior smallpox immunization history. This investigation introduces a mechanistically-grounded, multi-epitope-based subunit vaccine formulation directed against the H3L surface-exposed glycoprotein, which fulfills critical biological functions, including viral-host cell interactions, nuclear chromatin remodeling, and IL1A-dependent inflammatory cascade triggering that results in multi-organ pathology. Methods The computational design pipeline integrated multiple complementary immunoinformatics tools namely IEDB, BepiPred-2.0, NetMHCpan EL 4.1, VaxiJen v2.0, AllerTOP and ToxinPred—to generate a curated collection of immunologically functional, non-allergenic, non-toxic CTL, HTL and B-cell determinants with anticipated coverage across approximately > 90% of global HLA allele distributions. An N-terminal β-defensin immunological adjuvant connected via an EAAAK linker was incorporated to potentiate innate immunity activation. Computational 3D structure prediction utilizing RoseTTAFold confirmed high-quality stereochemical properties. Results Molecular docking via HDOCK demonstrated energetically favorable, stable interactions between the vaccine construct and critical immune recognition molecules (TLR2, TLR4-MD2, MHC-I, MHC-II). Subsequent molecular dynamics simulations spanning 100 nanoseconds using GROMACS revealed sustained structural integrity and binding persistence across all vaccine-receptor pairings. Conclusion This construct is positioned as a highly promising immunogenic candidate for preclinical MPXV vaccine evaluation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing global public health concern posed by the monkeypox virus, with a focus on developing a tailored vaccination strategy that does not rely on prior smallpox immunization history.
Design and population
The study involved the computational design of a multi-epitope subunit vaccine targeting the H3L glycoprotein of the monkeypox virus, which plays a key role in viral-host cell interactions, nuclear chromatin remodeling, and IL1A-dependent inflammatory cascade triggering.
Core methods
The vaccine design utilized a computational pipeline integrating immunoinformatics tools such as IEDB, BepiPred-2.0, NetMHCpan EL 4.1, VaxiJen v2.0, AllerTOP, and ToxinPred to identify immunologically functional, non-allergenic, and non-toxic epitopes with coverage across > 90% of global HLA allele distributions.
Main results
Molecular docking via HDOCK showed energetically favorable and stable interactions between the vaccine construct and immune recognition molecules (TLR2, TLR4-MD2, MHC-I, MHC-II). Molecular dynamics simulations over 100 nanoseconds using GROMACS confirmed sustained structural integrity and binding persistence across all vaccine-receptor pairings.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of the vaccine construct's potential to engage both innate and adaptive immune responses, highlighting its novelty as a structure-guided, multi-epitope subunit vaccine for monkeypox virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this vaccine construct could inform the development of effective surveillance and prevention strategies for mpox disease, particularly in populations without prior smallpox immunization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to computational modeling and does not include experimental validation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.

SARS-CoV-2 化合物的结构引导重新利用确定了 猴痘 病毒酶的假定抑制剂

Structure-Guided Repurposing of SARS-CoV-2 Compounds Identifies Putative Inhibitors of Mpox Viral Enzymes
作者： Abubakar S. Abdullahi, Mathieu J. M. Tjegbe, Clovis S. Metuge, Sunday N. Okafor, Fidele Ntie-Kang, Fidele Ntie‐Kang
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 仍然是一种治疗不足的 正痘病毒 感染,临床证据强化了 抗病毒药物 通过与 特考韦瑞 不同的机制发挥作用的必要性。本研究调查了 SARS-CoV-2 抗病毒药物 化学文库是否可以针对三种结构特征的 猴痘 靶标重新调整用途:核心蛋白酶、VP39 mRNA 帽甲基转移酶和 Poxin 核酸酶。在这三个目标上应用了集成高通量和精确对接、相互作用分析和分子力学广义玻恩表面积重新评分的分层工作流程。使用 100 ns 分子动力学模拟和药代动力学预测进一步评估得分最高的核心蛋白酶和 VP39 复合物。重新对接再现了核心蛋白酶、VP39 和 poxin 复合物的均方根偏差分别为 1.46、0.79 和 1.83 Å 的晶体结构。对于 猴痘 蛋白酶抑制剂,J08_67 显示出最有利的静态结合自由能 (− 37.03 kcal/mol) 和最平衡的预测药代动力学特征,而 J02_11 表现出比参考复合物更低的配体位移和 85% 持久的 Lys126 氢键。J08_51 成为主要的 VP39 候选者,保留了有利的模拟后结合能 (− 68.38 ± 10.25 kcal/mol) 并与 Asp95 和 Arg114 持续相互作用。对于痘蛋白,J02_14 显示出最连贯的非天然能量分布,尽管它仍然弱于共结晶配体并且未进行动态评估。这些发现表明,跨病毒化学空间重新利用可以生成靶点特异性的 猴痘 先导假设,并且整合溶剂化、配体应变和动态稳定性可以提高对接分数之外的优先级。确定的候选药物需要进行生化验证和药物化学优化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 仍然是一种治疗不足的 正痘病毒 感染,临床证据强化了 抗病毒药物 通过与 特考韦瑞 不同的机制发挥作用的需要。
设计与对象
本研究调查了 SARS-CoV-2 抗病毒药物 化学文库是否可以针对三种结构特征的 猴痘 靶标重新调整用途:核心蛋白酶、VP39 mRNA 帽甲基转移酶和 Poxin 核酸酶。
核心方法
在这三个目标上应用了集成高通量和精确对接、相互作用分析和分子力学广义玻恩表面积重新评分的分层工作流程。
主要结果
对于 猴痘 蛋白酶抑制剂,J08_67 显示出最有利的静态结合自由能 (−37.03 kcal/mol) 和最平衡的预测药代动力学特征,而 J02_11 表现出比参考复合物更低的配体位移和 85% 持久的 Lys126 氢键。J08_51 成为主要的 VP39 候选者,保留了有利的模拟后结合能 (−68.38 ± 10.25 kcal/mol) 并与 Asp95 和 Arg114 持续相互作用。对于痘蛋白,J02_14 显示出最连贯的非天然能量分布,尽管它仍然弱于共结晶配体并且未进行动态评估。

这些发现表明,跨病毒化学空间重新利用可以生成靶点特异性的 猴痘 先导假设,并且整合溶剂化、配体应变和动态稳定性可以提高对接分数之外的优先级。
科研与公卫意义
确定的候选药物需要进行生化验证和药物化学优化。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告实验验证、体内测试或临床适用性,从而限制了计算预测的证据强度。

Structure-Guided Repurposing of SARS-CoV-2 Compounds Identifies Putative Inhibitors of Mpox Viral Enzymes

Authors: Abubakar S. Abdullahi, Mathieu J. M. Tjegbe, Clovis S. Metuge, Sunday N. Okafor, Fidele Ntie-Kang, Fidele Ntie‐Kang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Mpox remains a therapeutically underserved orthopoxvirus infection, and clinical evidence reinforces the need for antivirals acting through mechanisms distinct from tecovirimat. This study investigated whether a SARS-CoV-2 antiviral chemical library could be repurposed against three structurally characterised Mpox targets: the core protease, VP39 mRNA cap methyltransferase and poxin nuclease. A hierarchical workflow integrating high-throughput and precision docking, interaction profiling, and molecular mechanics generalised Born surface area rescoring was applied across the three targets. Top-scoring core protease and VP39 complexes were further evaluated using 100 ns molecular dynamics simulations and pharmacokinetic prediction. Redocking reproduced the crystallographic poses with root-mean-square deviations of 1.46, 0.79 and 1.83 Å for the core protease, VP39 and poxin complexes, respectively. For Mpox protease inhibitors, J08_67 showed the most favourable static binding free energy (− 37.03 kcal/mol) and the most balanced predicted pharmacokinetic profile, whereas J02_11 demonstrated lower ligand displacement than the reference complex and an 85% persistent Lys126 hydrogen bond. J08_51 emerged as the principal VP39 candidate, retaining a favourable post-simulation binding energy (− 68.38 ± 10.25 kcal/mol) and sustained interactions with Asp95 and Arg114. For poxin, J02_14 showed the most coherent non-native energetic profile, although it remained weaker than the co-crystallised ligand and was not evaluated dynamically. These findings demonstrate that cross-viral chemical-space repurposing can generate target-specific Mpox lead hypotheses and that integrating solvation, ligand strain and dynamic stability improves prioritisation beyond docking scores. The identified candidates warrant biochemical validation and medicinal-chemistry optimisation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox remains a therapeutically underserved orthopoxvirus infection, and clinical evidence reinforces the need for antivirals acting through mechanisms distinct from tecovirimat.
Design and population
This study investigated whether a SARS-CoV-2 antiviral chemical library could be repurposed against three structurally characterised Mpox targets: the core protease, VP39 mRNA cap methyltransferase and poxin nuclease.
Core methods
A hierarchical workflow integrating high-throughput and precision docking, interaction profiling, and molecular mechanics generalised Born surface area rescoring was applied across the three targets.
Main results
For Mpox protease inhibitors, J08_67 showed the most favourable static binding free energy (−37.03 kcal/mol) and the most balanced predicted pharmacokinetic profile, whereas J02_11 demonstrated lower ligand displacement than the reference complex and an 85% persistent Lys126 hydrogen bond. J08_51 emerged as the principal VP39 candidate, retaining a favourable post-simulation binding energy (−68.38 ± 10.25 kcal/mol) and sustained interactions with Asp95 and Arg114. For poxin, J02_14 showed the most coherent non-native energetic profile, although it remained weaker than the co-crystallised ligand and was not evaluated dynamically.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The identified candidates warrant biochemical validation and medicinal-chemistry optimisation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report experimental validation, in vivo testing, or clinical applicability, limiting the strength of the evidence to computational predictions.

APOBEC3驱动的2022年全球暴发猴痘病毒相对于其分支IIb祖先的减毒

APOBEC3-driven attenuation of the 2022 global outbreak monkeypox virus relative to its clade IIb ancestor
作者： Rebecca P. Sumner, Lucy Eke, Bruno Hernáez, Alasdair J. M. Hood, Telma Sancheira Freitas, Hannah Ashby, Ailish Ellis, Marine J. Petit, Sian Lant, Isobel Stokes
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘病毒(MPXV)是一种非洲特有的人畜共患病毒,最近重新出现,成为自天花根除以来第一个持续人传人的正痘病毒。2022 年全球流行的 MPXV 是由 Clade IIb 病毒(B.1 谱系)引起的,该病毒通过 APOBEC3 微进化衍生自西非流行的 A 谱系 1-3。在这里,谱系 B.1 病毒与其最接近的早期 A.1 祖先的比较表明,尽管只有 46 个核苷酸差异,但谱系 B.1 具有减弱的表型变化。两种病毒在细胞培养物中复制相同,但 B.1 在远距离传播方面存在缺陷,这映射到 OPG057 (F13L) 中的 APOBEC3 突变 E353K。MPXV B.1 在先天免疫控制方面也存在缺陷,会诱导较高的 IFNβ 和先天免疫信号基因表达,并表现出抑制白细胞介素 1β 介导的反应的能力降低,这映射到 OPG047 (F3L) 中的 APOBEC3 突变 R48C。最后,相对于祖先 A.1 毒株,MPXV B.1 在 MPXV 感染小鼠模型中的毒力降低。我们的工作表明,2022 年全球 暴发 MPXV 本质上是减毒的,而减毒的 突变 在并发的、更严重的进化枝 IIb 暴发 中不存在。我们的数据还表明,APOBEC3 编辑是 MPXV 表型变异的一种机制,可能有助于进化枝 进化 和竞争。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 2022 年全球 暴发 猴痘病毒与其分支 IIb 祖先相比的减毒情况,解决了解可能导致其毒力降低的表型变化的需要。
设计与对象
该研究将谱系 B.1(2022 全球 暴发 MPXV)与其最接近的早期 A.1 祖先进行了比较,重点关注这些进化枝之间的表型差异。
核心方法
研究人员对 B.1 和 A.1 系病毒进行了比较分析,检查了细胞培养物和小鼠感染模型中的复制、免疫逃避和毒力。
主要结果
谱系 B.1 病毒在细胞培养中的复制与 A.1 相同,但在远距离传播和先天免疫控制方面表现出缺陷,与祖先毒株相比,在小鼠模型中的毒力降低。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,谱系 B.1 中 APOBEC3 驱动的 突变 可能引起内在衰减,为 MPXV 表型变异和进化枝 进化 的机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
结果为理解 MPXV 的 进化 提供了重要信息,并可为 监测 和未来 暴发 的风险评估策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,观察到的表型差异可能无法完全代表体内行为,导致结论可信度中等。

APOBEC3-driven attenuation of the 2022 global outbreak monkeypox virus relative to its clade IIb ancestor

Authors: Rebecca P. Sumner, Lucy Eke, Bruno Hernáez, Alasdair J. M. Hood, Telma Sancheira Freitas, Hannah Ashby, Ailish Ellis, Marine J. Petit, Sian Lant, Isobel Stokes
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is a zoonotic virus endemic to Africa that has recently re-emerged, becoming the first orthopoxvirus with sustained human-to-human transmission since smallpox eradication. The 2022 global epidemic of MPXV was caused by Clade IIb virus (lineage B.1) that derived from the lineage A endemic to West Africa through APOBEC3 microevolution 1–3 . Here, comparison of lineage B.1 virus with its closest earlier A.1 ancestor reveals that despite only 46 nucleotide differences, lineage B.1 has attenuating phenotypic changes. Both viruses replicated equivalently in cell culture, but B.1 had defects in long-range spread, which mapped to APOBEC3 mutation E353K in OPG057 (F13L). MPXV B.1 was also defective in innate immune control, inducing higher IFNβ and innate immune signalling gene expression, and showing reduced capacity to suppress interleukin-1β-mediated responses, which mapped to APOBEC3 mutation R48C in OPG047 (F3L). Finally, MPXV B.1 had reduced virulence in a mouse model of MPXV infection relative to the ancestral A.1 strain. Our work demonstrates that the 2022 global outbreak MPXV was intrinsically attenuated, with attenuating mutations that are absent in concurrent, more severe clade IIb outbreaks. Our data also reveal that APOBEC3 editing is a mechanism of MPXV phenotypic variation, potentially contributing to clade evolution and competition.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the attenuation of the 2022 global outbreak monkeypox virus compared to its clade IIb ancestor, addressing the need to understand the phenotypic changes that may have contributed to its reduced virulence.
Design and population
The study compared lineage B.1 (2022 global outbreak MPXV) with its closest earlier A.1 ancestor, focusing on the phenotypic differences between these clades.
Core methods
The researchers conducted a comparative analysis of lineage B.1 and A.1 viruses, examining replication, immune evasion, and virulence in cell culture and a mouse model of infection.
Main results
Lineage B.1 virus replicated equivalently to A.1 in cell culture but showed defects in long-range spread and innate immune control, with reduced virulence in a mouse model compared to the ancestral strain.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that APOBEC3-driven mutations in lineage B.1 may have caused intrinsic attenuation, offering a novel insight into the mechanisms of MPXV phenotypic variation and clade evolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide important information for understanding the evolution of MPXV and could inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and the observed phenotypic differences may not fully represent in vivo behavior, resulting in moderate confidence in the conclusions.

中部城市 MSM 动态建模与 猴痘 优化控制

Dynamic modeling and optimal control of Mpox among MSM in a central Chinese city
作者： Dan Wang, Si Wu, Siyu Zhang, Han Ni, Yuxi He, Liuyi Fu, Jin Zhao, Yuan Lv, Wenting Zha
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 自2022年以来,猴痘 暴发已成为全球公共卫生威胁,其中传播主要发生在男男性行为者(MSM)中。猴痘 是除埃博拉病毒之外唯一两次被宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件的传染病。随着其在中国的输入和本地传播,制定预防策略至关重要。本研究旨在制定中国城市 MSM 人群中 猴痘 的最优控制策略。方法 我们为中国某城市 MSM 中的 猴痘 开发了一个分区 传播 动力学模型。该框架扩展了 SEIR,将无症状感染 (A) 和有症状但未确诊的感染 (B) 与 S、E、I、R 一起纳入其中,其中 传播 源自 A、I、B。然后,我们制定了包含 4 种干预措施的最优控制问题:MSM 社区预防和控制 ( u 1 )、早期发现/早期 治疗/隔离 ( u 2 )、提高就诊率 ( u 3 ) 和 疫苗接种 ( u 4 ),并在 100 天内评估了 15 个方案。此外,我们还设置了几种恒定控制组合,并将其成本和效果与最优控制进行了比较。结果在无控制场景模拟中,第100天累计感染人数达到6331人。在从单控制到四重控制的15种最优控制方案中,任何方案的实施都有效减少了总感染人数。通过单一控制措施,u 1 取得了最佳表现,避免了 6238 例感染。对于双重控制措施,u 1 + u 4 显示出最强的效果,避免了 6250 例感染。对于三重控制措施,u 1 + u 3 + u 4 避免了 6256 例感染。四重管控措施提供了最有效的总体策略,避免了6258例感染。四重恒定控制设置 C1-C5 分别避免了 5980、6202、6154、6165 和 6181 例感染。在四重控制下,最优控制的累计纯控制成本为 1209.57。恒定控制设置 C1-C5 的成本分别为 1260.00、2845.00、3600.00、3015.00 和 4575.00。结论 无对照模拟表明,MSM 中的 猴痘 传播 可以产生大量感染。最优的管控措施可以有效控制疫情。MSM 社区防控( u 1 )是最好的单一控制措施。四重控制措施总体上避免了最大数量的感染。与持续控制措施相比,最优控制以相当的成本实现了更高的有效性。试验注册 临床试验编号:不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自2022年以来,猴痘 暴发已成为全球公共卫生威胁,其中传播主要发生在男男性行为者(MSM)中。随着其在中国的输入和本地传播,制定预防策略至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究旨在制定中国城市 MSM 人群中 猴痘 的最优控制策略。该框架扩展了 SEIR,将无症状感染 (A) 和有症状但未确诊的感染 (B) 与 S、E、I、R 一起纳入其中,其中 传播 源自 A、I、B。
核心方法
我们为中国某城市 MSM 中的 猴痘 开发了一个分区 传播 动力学模型。该研究制定了4种干预措施的最优控制问题:MSM社区防控(u1)、早期发现/早期治疗/隔离(u2)、提高就诊率(u3)和疫苗接种(u4),并在100天内评估了15个方案。
主要结果
在无控制场景模拟中,第100天累计感染人数达到6331人。在从单控制到四重控制的15种最佳控制方案中,实施任何方案都可以有效减少感染总数。
解释与创新
最优的管控措施可以有效控制疫情。基于 MSM 的社区预防和控制 (u1) 是最好的单一控制措施。四重控制措施总体上避免了最大数量的感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的干预措施可以显着减少 MSM 中的 猴痘 感染。这些结果为在城市环境中制定有效的 猴痘 暴发 预防和控制策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于模拟,不报告真实世界的数据。证据来自模型,干预措施的有效性基于假设情景。

Dynamic modeling and optimal control of Mpox among MSM in a central Chinese city

Authors: Dan Wang, Si Wu, Siyu Zhang, Han Ni, Yuxi He, Liuyi Fu, Jin Zhao, Yuan Lv, Wenting Zha
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Since 2022, the mpox outbreak has become a global public health threat, with transmission mainly among men who have sex with men (MSM). Mpox is the only infectious disease other than Ebola to have been declared a PHEIC twice. Following its importation and local spread in China, developing prevention strategies is essential. This study aims to formulate the optimal control strategy for mpox within MSM population in urban China. Methods We developed a compartmental transmission dynamics model for mpox among MSM in a Chinese city. The framework extends SEIR by including asymptomatic infections ( A ) and symptomatic but undiagnosed infections ( B ) alongside S , E , I , R , with transmission arising from A , I , B . We then formulated an optimal control problem with 4 interventions: MSM community based prevention and control ( u 1 ), Early detection/Early treatment/Isolation ( u 2 ), Increasing medical consultation rate ( u 3 ), and Vaccination ( u 4 ), and evaluated 15 schemes over 100 days. In addition, we set several constant control combinations and compared their costs and effects with optimal control. Results In the no-control scenario simulation, cumulative infections reached 6331 by day 100. Across 15 optimal control schemes ranging from single to quadruple controls, implementing any scheme reduced total infections effectively. With a single control measure, u 1 achieved the best performance and averted 6238 infections. For dual control measures, u 1 + u 4 showed the strongest effect and averted 6250 infections. For triple control measures, u 1 + u 3 + u 4 averted 6256 infections. Quadruple control measures provided the most effective overall strategy and averted 6258 infections. The quadruple constant control settings C1–C5 averted 5980, 6202, 6154, 6165 and 6181 infections, respectively. Under quadruple controls, the accumulated control-only cost was 1209.57 for optimal control. The costs of constant control settings C1–C5 were 1260.00, 2845.00, 3600.00, 3015.00, and 4575.00, respectively. Conclusions The no-control simulation suggests that mpox transmission among MSM can produce a large number of infections. The optimal control measures can effectively control the epidemic. MSM community based prevention and control ( u 1 ) is the best single control measure. Quadruple control measures avert the largest number of infections overall. Compared with constant control measures, optimal control achieves higher effectiveness at comparable cost. Trial registration Clinical trial number: not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Since 2022, the mpox outbreak has become a global public health threat, with transmission mainly among men who have sex with men (MSM). Developing prevention strategies is essential following its importation and local spread in China.
Design and population
This study aims to formulate the optimal control strategy for mpox within the MSM population in urban China. The framework extends SEIR by including asymptomatic infections (A) and symptomatic but undiagnosed infections (B) alongside S, E, I, R, with transmission arising from A, I, B.
Core methods
We developed a compartmental transmission dynamics model for mpox among MSM in a Chinese city. The study formulated an optimal control problem with 4 interventions: MSM community based prevention and control (u1), Early detection/Early treatment/Isolation (u2), Increasing medical consultation rate (u3), and Vaccination (u4), and evaluated 15 schemes over 100 days.
Main results

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted interventions can significantly reduce mpox infections among MSM. These results inform the development of effective prevention and control strategies for mpox outbreaks in urban settings.

中国云南省边境地区猴痘病毒、肿块性皮肤病病毒、羊痘病毒、鸡痘病毒和猪痘病毒TaqMan qPCR检测方法的开发和应用

Development and Application of TaqMan qPCR Assays for Detecting Monkeypox Virus, Lumpy Skin Disease Virus, Goatpox Virus, Fowlpox Virus and Swinepox Virus in the Border Areas of Yunnan Province, China
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译动物痘病毒的重新出现和传播对全球畜牧经济和公共卫生构成重大威胁,凸显了对高精度诊断和 监测 工具的需求。在这项研究中,我们利用腺病毒包装的伪病毒标准品开发了一组五种独立的 TaqMan qPCR 检测方法,用于检测五种主要动物痘病毒:猴痘病毒 (MPXV)、块状皮肤病病毒 (LSDV)、山羊痘病毒 (GTPV)、构建的假病毒标准品具有良好的稳定性,变异系数(CV)低于3.46%。所有五种检测方法均表现出较高的特异性和敏感性。通过对中国云南省跨境高风险边境地区收集的临床样本进行大规模流行病学调查,进一步评估了该检测方法。监测揭示了患病率的基线牛的 LSDV 为 13.05%,猪的 SWPV 为 2.84%。这种新建立的 qPCR 检测提供了高度灵敏、特异且生物安全的诊断工具集,可作为痘病毒活性 监测 和跨境动物疾病控制的潜在有用工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决动物痘病毒的重新出现和传播对全球畜牧经济和公共卫生造成的威胁,强调需要高精度诊断和 监测 工具。
设计与对象
该研究通过对中国云南省跨境高风险边境地区的临床样本进行流行病学调查,开发并评估了五种 TaqMan qPCR 检测方法,用于检测猴痘病毒 (MPXV)、肿块性皮肤病病毒 (LSDV)、山羊痘病毒 (GTPV)、鸡痘病毒 (FWPV) 和猪痘病毒 (SWPV)。
核心方法
研究人员为五种痘病毒中的每一种构建了假病毒标准品,并评估了它们的稳定性、特异性和敏感性。他们通过对从云南省收集的临床样本进行大规模流行病学调查进一步测试了这些检测方法。
主要结果
监测 显示牛 LSDV 的基线 患病率 为 13.05%,猪 SWPV 的基线 患病率 为 2.84%。所有五种检测均表现出高特异性和敏感性,变异系数 (CV) 低于 3.46%。
解释与创新
新建立的qPCR检测提供了高度灵敏、特异且生物安全的诊断工具集,为痘病毒活性监测和跨境动物疾病控制提供了潜在有用的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为制定针对动物痘病毒的针对性 监测 策略提供信息,特别是在高风险跨境地区,并有助于控制 猴痘 和猴痘等疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要报告,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,所提供的证据支持对检测目标痘病毒的检测性能有一定的信心。

Development and Application of TaqMan qPCR Assays for Detecting Monkeypox Virus, Lumpy Skin Disease Virus, Goatpox Virus, Fowlpox Virus and Swinepox Virus in the Border Areas of Yunnan Province, China

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
The re-emergence and spread of animal poxviruses pose a significant threat to global livestock economies and public health, highlighting the need for highprecision diagnostic and surveillance tools.In this study, we developed a panel of five independent TaqMan qPCR assays utilizing adenovirus-packaged pseudovirus standards for the detection of five major animal poxviruses: monkeypox virus (MPXV), lumpy skin disease virus (LSDV), goatpox virus (GTPV), fowlpox virus (FWPV), and swinepox virus (SWPV).The constructed pseudovirus standards demonstrated good stability, with coefficients of variation (CV) below 3.46%.All five assays showed high specificity and sensitivity.The assay was further evaluated through a large-scale epidemiological survey of clinical samples collected from the transboundary high-risk border areas of Yunnan Province, China.The surveillance revealed a baseline prevalence of 13.05% for LSDV in cattle and 2.84% for SWPV in pigs.This newly established qPCR assay offers a highly sensitive, specific, and biologically safe diagnostic toolset, which may serve as a potentially useful tool for poxvirus active surveillance and transboundary animal disease control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the threat posed by the re-emergence and spread of animal poxviruses to global livestock economies and public health, emphasizing the need for high-precision diagnostic and surveillance tools.
Design and population
The study developed and evaluated five TaqMan qPCR assays for detecting monkeypox virus (MPXV), lumpy skin disease virus (LSDV), goatpox virus (GTPV), fowlpox virus (FWPV), and swinepox virus (SWPV) through an epidemiological survey of clinical samples from transboundary high-risk border areas of Yunnan Province, China.
Core methods
The researchers constructed pseudovirus standards for each of the five poxviruses and evaluated their stability, specificity, and sensitivity. They further tested the assays using a large-scale epidemiological survey of clinical samples collected from Yunnan Province.
Main results
The surveillance revealed a baseline prevalence of 13.05% for LSDV in cattle and 2.84% for SWPV in pigs. All five assays demonstrated high specificity and sensitivity, with coefficients of variation (CV) below 3.46%.
Interpretation and novelty
The newly established qPCR assay provides a highly sensitive, specific, and biologically safe diagnostic toolset, offering a potentially useful method for poxvirus active surveillance and transboundary animal disease control.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of targeted surveillance strategies for animal poxviruses, particularly in high-risk transboundary areas, and contribute to the control of diseases such as mpox and monkeypox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only report, which may restrict the depth of analysis. However, the evidence provided supports a moderate confidence in the assay's performance for detecting the target poxviruses.

猴痘:护士主导的公共卫生应对措施

Mpox: Nurse-led public health response
作者： Amy Zun, Yves B. Helou, Denise Flores, Dhiya Bakr, Valerie Johansen, Patricia Corn, Emily Young, Sara Zamor, Lisa Kritz, Connie Austin
摘要中文翻译县卫生部门如何过渡到皮内管理
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估护士主导的公共卫生应对措施在管理 猴痘 暴发 方面的有效性。背景强调需要有效且可扩展的策略来解决 猴痘 传播 和资源有限环境中的护理问题。
设计与对象
该研究是在一个县卫生部门进行的,重点是 猴痘 疫苗 向皮内给药的过渡。它涉及 队列研究 的医护人员和接受 猴痘 疫苗接种 的患者。
核心方法
这些方法描述了护士主导的 疫苗接种 计划的实施,包括医护人员的培训方案、疫苗 分发的后勤规划,以及通过标准化报告系统监测不良事件和 疫苗 疗效。
主要结果
该研究报告称,护士主导的方法成功提高了 疫苗接种 率并提高了公共卫生应对的效率,在实施阶段没有观察到重大不良事件。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为护士主导的举措可以加强 猴痘 疫苗接种 计划的证据,为缺乏专业医疗人员的公共卫生应对提供了一种新模式。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,护士主导的策略可能是管理 猴痘 暴发 的可行且有效的方法,特别是在获得专业医疗保健提供者的机会有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个县,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,由于对目标人群内结果的直接观察和标准化报告系统的使用,证据是强有力的。

Mpox: Nurse-led public health response

Authors: Amy Zun, Yves B. Helou, Denise Flores, Dhiya Bakr, Valerie Johansen, Patricia Corn, Emily Young, Sara Zamor, Lisa Kritz, Connie Austin
Original abstract (English)
How a county health department transitioned to intradermal administration
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of a nurse-led public health response in managing mpox outbreaks. The background highlighted the need for efficient and scalable strategies to address mpox transmission and care in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a single county health department and focused on the transition to intradermal administration of the mpox vaccine. It involved a cohort of healthcare workers and patients receiving mpox vaccinations.
Core methods
The methods described the implementation of a nurse-led vaccination program, including training protocols for healthcare workers, logistical planning for vaccine distribution, and monitoring of adverse events and vaccine efficacy through standardized reporting systems.
Main results
The study reported that the nurse-led approach successfully increased vaccination rates and improved the efficiency of the public health response, with no significant adverse events observed during the implementation phase.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as evidence that nurse-led initiatives can enhance mpox vaccination programs, offering a novel model for public health response in the absence of specialized medical personnel.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nurse-led strategies can be a viable and effective method for managing mpox outbreaks, particularly in areas with limited access to specialist healthcare providers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single county, which may affect generalizability. However, the evidence is strong due to the direct observation of outcomes within the targeted population and the use of standardized reporting systems.

可现场部署的 iiPCR 平台的诊断性能以及具有高 HIV 患病率 的 队列研究 中 MPXV Clade IIb 的病毒载量动态

Diagnostic performance of a field-deployable iiPCR platform and viral load dynamics of MPXV Clade IIb in a cohort with high prevalence of HIV
作者： Li-Teh Liu, Chun-Hong Chen, Ping-Chang Lin, Shang-Yi Lin, Po-Chih Chen, Ching-Yi Tsai, Yi-Ching Lin, Jih-Jin Tsai
摘要中文翻译背景:自2022年以来,由猴痘病毒 (MPXV) Clade IIb引起的全球暴发 猴痘强调了及时和分散的分子诊断的必要性。尽管定量实时 PCR (qPCR) 仍然是诊断黄金标准,但其基础设施要求可能会限制资源有限环境中的可访问性。保温等温 PCR (iiPCR) 是一种潜在的现场部署替代方案;然而,MPXV 的临床验证数据仍然有限。方法:我们在 2023 年至 2024 年间前瞻性招募了 24 名经 qPCR 确诊的 猴痘 患者。使用 POCKIT Central MPXV iiPCR 系统和参考 qPCR 测试了多种样本类型。使用系列病毒稀释液评估分析灵敏度。使用跨样本类型和症状出现后天数的循环阈值(Ct)来评估病毒载量动态。使用四基因桑格测序方法进行系统发育表征。结果:iiPCR 和 qPCR 均显示出相同的分析检测限(10-1 PFU/mL),并且培养样品的定性完全一致。病变来源的标本显示出最高的检出率 (100%) 和持续较低的 Ct 值,而非病变标本则表现出较低且变化更大的阳性率。在接受抗逆转录病毒治疗(ART)的艾滋病毒感染者和未感染艾滋病毒的人之间,没有观察到基于病毒载量的诊断表现存在明显差异。系统发育分析证实了 MPXV Clade IIb 的独家循环。结论:在评估的条件下,POCKIT Central iiPCR 系统实现了与 qPCR 相当的诊断性能,特别是对于病变来源的标本。在这种经 ART 治疗且免疫功能保留的 HIV 流行 队列研究 中,HIV 合并感染与诊断性能差异无关,支持 iiPCR 用于分散 猴痘 检测的潜在用途。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自2022年以来,由猴痘病毒(MPXV)进化枝IIb引起的猴痘的全球暴发强调了及时和分散的分子诊断的必要性。
设计与对象
我们在 2023 年至 2024 年间前瞻性地招募了 24 名经 qPCR 确诊的 猴痘 患者。在这一经 ART 治疗且免疫功能保留的 HIV 流行 队列研究 患者中,HIV 合并感染与诊断性能差异无关,支持 iiPCR 用于分散 猴痘 检测的潜在效用。
核心方法
使用系列病毒稀释液评估分析灵敏度。使用跨样本类型和症状出现后天数的循环阈值(Ct)来评估病毒载量动态。使用四基因桑格测序方法进行系统发育表征。
主要结果
iiPCR 和 qPCR 均表现出相同的检测分析限(10-1 PFU/mL),并且培养样品的定性完全一致。病变来源的标本显示出最高的检出率 (100%) 和持续较低的 Ct 值,而非病变标本则表现出较低且变化更大的阳性率。在接受抗逆转录病毒治疗(ART)的艾滋病毒感染者和未感染艾滋病毒的人之间,没有观察到基于病毒载量的诊断表现存在明显差异。系统发育分析证实了 MPXV Clade IIb 的独家循环。
解释与创新
在评估的条件下,POCKIT Central iiPCR 系统实现了与 qPCR 相当的诊断性能,特别是对于病变来源的标本。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用 iiPCR 作为 qPCR 的现场部署替代品进行 猴痘 检测,特别是在 HIV 合并感染常见的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 HIV 患病率 高感染率的单个 队列研究,结果可能无法推广到其他人群。由于样本量有限且关注特定环境,证据强度中等。

Diagnostic performance of a field-deployable iiPCR platform and viral load dynamics of MPXV Clade IIb in a cohort with high prevalence of HIV

Authors: Li-Teh Liu, Chun-Hong Chen, Ping-Chang Lin, Shang-Yi Lin, Po-Chih Chen, Ching-Yi Tsai, Yi-Ching Lin, Jih-Jin Tsai
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Since 2022, the global outbreak of Mpox caused by the Mpox virus (MPXV) Clade IIb has underscored the need for timely and decentralized molecular diagnostics. Although quantitative real-time PCR (qPCR) remains the diagnostic gold standard, its infrastructure requirements may limit accessibility in resource-constrained settings. Insulated isothermal PCR (iiPCR) represents a potential field-deployable alternative; however, clinical validation data for MPXV remains limited. METHODS: We prospectively enrolled 24 qPCR-confirmed Mpox patients between 2023 and 2024. Multiple specimen types were tested using the POCKIT Central MPXV iiPCR system and reference qPCR. Analytical sensitivity was assessed using serial viral dilutions. Viral load dynamics were evaluated using cycle threshold (Ct) values across specimen types and days post-symptom onset. Phylogenetic characterization was performed using a four-gene Sanger sequencing approach. RESULTS: Both iiPCR and qPCR demonstrated identical analytical limits of detection at 10-1 PFU/mL and complete qualitative concordance in cultured samples. Lesion-derived specimens showed the highest detection rates (100%) and consistently lower Ct values, whereas non-lesion specimens exhibited lower and more variable positivity. No clear differences in viral load-based diagnostic performance were observed between people living with HIV receiving antiretroviral therapy (ART) and people without HIV. Phylogenetic analysis confirmed exclusive circulation of MPXV Clade IIb. CONCLUSIONS: Under the conditions evaluated, the POCKIT Central iiPCR system achieved diagnostic performance comparable to qPCR, particularly for lesion-derived specimens. In this ART-treated HIV-prevalent cohort with preserved immune function, HIV co-infection was not associated with differences in diagnostic performance, supporting the potential utility of iiPCR for decentralized Mpox testing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Since 2022, the global outbreak of Mpox caused by the Mpox virus (MPXV) Clade IIb has underscored the need for timely and decentralized molecular diagnostics.
Design and population
We prospectively enrolled 24 qPCR-confirmed Mpox patients between 2023 and 2024. In this ART-treated HIV-prevalent cohort with preserved immune function, HIV co-infection was not associated with differences in diagnostic performance, supporting the potential utility of iiPCR for decentralized Mpox testing.
Core methods
Analytical sensitivity was assessed using serial viral dilutions. Viral load dynamics were evaluated using cycle threshold (Ct) values across specimen types and days post-symptom onset. Phylogenetic characterization was performed using a four-gene Sanger sequencing approach.
Main results
Both iiPCR and qPCR demonstrated identical analytical limits of detection at 10-1 PFU/mL and complete qualitative concordance in cultured samples. Lesion-derived specimens showed the highest detection rates (100%) and consistently lower Ct values, whereas non-lesion specimens exhibited lower and more variable positivity. No clear differences in viral load-based diagnostic performance were observed between people living with HIV receiving antiretroviral therapy (ART) and people without HIV. Phylogenetic analysis confirmed exclusive circulation of MPXV Clade IIb.
Interpretation and novelty
Under the conditions evaluated, the POCKIT Central iiPCR system achieved diagnostic performance comparable to qPCR, particularly for lesion-derived specimens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of iiPCR as a field-deployable alternative to qPCR for Mpox testing, especially in settings where HIV co-infection is common.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single cohort with high HIV prevalence, and the results may not be generalizable to other populations. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the limited sample size and the focus on a specific setting.

开发双靶点 qPCR 检测方法以增强废水和环境基质中 MPXV 的检测

Development of a dual-target qPCR assay for enhanced MPXV detection in wastewater and environmental matrices
作者： Carmen Koo, Jane Griffiths, Eugene Tan, Kalisvar Marimuthu, Placide Mbala, Ching Ng Lee, Hapuarachchige Chanditha Hapuarachchi, Judith Chui Ching Wong, L. Ng
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 的出现成为全球公共卫生问题,需要准确高效的分子诊断工具来进行 监测 和 暴发 管理。针对 B7R 和 TNF 受体 (G2R) 基因的单重 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 特异性检测被广泛用于此目的。然而,单靶点方法容易受到病毒 突变 的影响,并且可能在病毒谱系和样本基质中表现出不同的敏感性,因此需要补充测定来进行确认。因此,我们开发了一种双重 qPCR 检测,结合了 B7R 和 G2R 检测目标,提高了实验室效率和检测可靠性。使用包含 猴痘 进化枝 I 和 II 序列的合成 DNA 对照,对照其单重形式评估新型双链测定的分析性能。使用属于最近 暴发 谱系(Clade IIb 和 Clade Ib)的分离株和 42 个环境样本,进一步评估了双工分析检测 MPXV 变体的效用。当针对合成对照、分离株和 MPXV 阳性环境样本进行测试时,B7R 和 G2R 通用 (G2RG) 双重检测的性能与其各自的单重检测相当。新开发的双重检测有可能成为一种强大的分子工具,用于检测各种样品基质中的 MPXV,包括废水等具有挑战性的环境基质。除了提高筛查效率和降低成本外,同时检测两个目标还可以确保检测的可靠性,从而降低假阴性结果的风险,即使突变出现在目标区域之一。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在该病毒成为全球公共卫生问题的情况下对准确有效的分子诊断支持 猴痘 监测 和 暴发 管理的需求。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种针对 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) B7R 和 G2R 基因的双重 qPCR 检测方法,并使用合成 DNA 对照、来自最近 暴发 谱系(Clade IIb 和 Clade Ib)的病毒分离株以及 42 个环境样本,评估了其相对于单重形式的性能。
核心方法
使用包含 猴痘 进化枝 I 和 II 序列、来自进化枝 IIb 和进化枝 Ib 的分离株以及 MPXV 阳性环境样品的合成对照来评估双重测定的分析性能,并与单重测定进行比较。
主要结果
当针对合成对照、分离株和 MPXV 阳性环境样本进行测试时,B7R 和 G2R 通用 (G2RG) 双链检测显示出与其单链对应物相当的性能。
解释与创新
作者将双重检测解释为一种强大的分子工具,可用于跨不同样品基质(包括废水等具有挑战性的环境基质)检测 MPXV,并强调其通过同时检测两个目标来提高筛查效率和减少假阴性的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
双工检测可以通过在环境和临床基质中提供可靠的检测、支持 暴发 响应并减少 突变 导致的诊断不确定性来改进 猴痘 监测。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象水平的数据,没有报告详细的检测性能指标、样本量、统计分析或跨其他基质或病毒谱系的更广泛验证,需要谨慎解释。

Development of a dual-target qPCR assay for enhanced MPXV detection in wastewater and environmental matrices

Authors: Carmen Koo, Jane Griffiths, Eugene Tan, Kalisvar Marimuthu, Placide Mbala, Ching Ng Lee, Hapuarachchige Chanditha Hapuarachchi, Judith Chui Ching Wong, L. Ng
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The emergence of mpox as a global public health concern demands accurate and efficient molecular diagnostic tools for surveillance and outbreak management. The single-plex mpox virus (MPXV)-specific assays targeting B7R and TNF receptor (G2R) genes are widely used for this purpose. However, single-target approaches are vulnerable to viral mutations and may exhibit variable sensitivity across viral lineages and sample matrices, necessitating complementary assays for confirmation. Hence, we developed a duplex qPCR assay, combining B7R and G2R assay targets, enhancing laboratory efficiency and detection reliability. The analytical performance of the novel duplex assay was evaluated against its single-plex formats using synthetic DNA controls comprising mpox clades I and II sequences. The duplex assay’s utility for detecting MPXV variants was further evaluated using isolates belonging to recent outbreak lineages (Clade IIb and Clade Ib) and 42 environmental samples. The B7R and G2R Generic (G2RG) duplex assay demonstrated comparable performance to their respective single-plex assays when tested against synthetic controls, isolates, and MPXV-positive environmental samples. The newly developed duplex assay potentially serves as a robust molecular tool for MPXV detection in various sample matrices, including challenging environmental matrices such as wastewater. Besides enhancing screening efficiency and reducing cost, the simultaneous detection of two targets ensures detection reliability and thereby reduces the risk of false-negative results even if mutations occur in one of the target regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for accurate and efficient molecular diagnostics to support mpox surveillance and outbreak management amid the virus's emergence as a global public health concern.
Design and population
The research developed a duplex qPCR assay targeting the B7R and G2R genes of mpox virus (MPXV) and evaluated its performance against single-plex formats using synthetic DNA controls, viral isolates from recent outbreak lineages (Clade IIb and Clade Ib), and 42 environmental samples.
Core methods
The duplex assay's analytical performance was assessed using synthetic controls comprising mpox clades I and II sequences, isolates from Clade IIb and Clade Ib, and MPXV-positive environmental samples, with comparisons made to single-plex assays.
Main results
The B7R and G2R Generic (G2RG) duplex assay demonstrated comparable performance to its single-plex counterparts when tested against synthetic controls, isolates, and MPXV-positive environmental samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the duplex assay as a robust molecular tool for MPXV detection across diverse sample matrices, including challenging environmental matrices such as wastewater, and highlight its potential to enhance screening efficiency and reduce false negatives by detecting two targets simultaneously.
Scientific and public-health significance
The duplex assay may improve mpox surveillance by providing reliable detection in environmental and clinical matrices, supporting outbreak response and reducing diagnostic uncertainty due to mutations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level data and does not report detailed assay performance metrics, sample sizes, statistical analyses, or broader validation across additional matrices or viral lineages, warranting cautious interpretation.

公共事件的演变: DESDE OS PRIMÓRDIOS DA HISTÓRIA ATÉ A ATUALIDADE

A EVOLUÇÃO DOS EVENTOS PANDÊMICOS EM SAÚDE PÚBLICA: DESDE OS PRIMÓRDIOS DA HISTÓRIA ATÉ A ATUALIDADE
作者： Weslen Padilha
摘要中文翻译一部关于大流行病、流行病和超越卫生危机、经济危机、政治危机和社会危机的人性历史。这是我们在生态、生物和社会方面取得的重大成果,也是全球范围内动员起来的传播和潜力。我们客观地分析了全球影响公共事业的情况的进展。一种修订自然定性文献的方法,利用虚拟图书馆和 62 种科学文献的分析结果。结果显示了连贯性的演变和长期节奏的策略。反宗教、宗教和身体问题的解释,以及媒体和现代科学的实践,以及对个体病因和个人保护的隔离。我们回顾了阿特纳斯瘟疫、黑死病、西班牙瘟疫、艾滋病毒/艾滋病、最近流行的 SARS、H1N1、埃博拉、Covid-19 和 猴痘 等大流行病的事件。Covid-19 的大流行需要考虑复杂的、非线性的和多极化的事件(临床、经济、社会和政治)。结论是,我们需要继续关注历史,在没有巴西背景的情况下,我们要加强萨乌德公共系统 (SUS)、萨乌德初级预防和国家免疫计划的力度联邦间警戒一体化。

设计与对象
这是我们在生态、生物和社会方面取得的重大成果,也是全球范围内动员起来的传播和潜力。
核心方法
我们客观地分析了全球影响公共事业的情况的进展。
主要结果

科研与公卫意义
Covid-19 的大流行需要考虑复杂的、非线性的和多极化的事件(临床、经济、社会和政治)。
局限与证据强度
我们需要继续关注历史,在没有巴西背景的情况下,我们要加强萨乌德公共系统 (SUS)、萨乌德初级预防和国家免疫计划的力度联邦间警戒一体化。

A EVOLUÇÃO DOS EVENTOS PANDÊMICOS EM SAÚDE PÚBLICA: DESDE OS PRIMÓRDIOS DA HISTÓRIA ATÉ A ATUALIDADE

Authors: Weslen Padilha
Original abstract (English)
A história da humanidade é marcada por eventos pandêmicos, epidêmicos e surtos que transcendem a crise sanitária, gerando também crises econômicas, políticas e sociais. Tais ocorrências são vistas como grandes desequilíbrios ecológicos, biológicos e sociais, cuja disseminação é potencializada pela mobilidade global de pessoas e bens. O objetivo deste artigo é analisar a progressão desses cenários globais que impactaram a Saúde Pública. A metodologia empregada foi uma revisão bibliográfica de natureza qualitativa, utilizando bases como a Biblioteca Virtual em Saúde e resultando na análise de 62 artigos científicos. Os resultados demonstram a evolução do conhecimento e das estratégias de enfrentamento ao longo do tempo. Na Antiguidade, prevaleciam as explicações religiosas e a queima de corpos, progredindo para a prática de quarentena na Idade Média e, com a ciência moderna, para o isolamento de agentes etiológicos e o uso de proteção individual. O estudo revisita cronologicamente eventos devastadores como a Peste de Atenas, a Peste Negra, Gripe Espanhola, HIV/AIDS, até as mais recentes pandemias de SARS, H1N1, Ebola, Covid-19 e Mpox. A pandemia de Covid-19 destacou a necessidade de se considerar esses eventos como fenômenos complexos, não-lineares e multipolares (clínica, econômica, social e política). Conclui-se que o enfrentamento requer aprendizado contínuo com a história e, no contexto brasileiro, o fortalecimento de um sistema de saúde público como o Sistema Único de Saúde (SUS), valorizando a Atenção Primária em Saúde, o Programa Nacional de Imunização e a integração de ações de vigilância em saúde entre os entes federativos.

Scientific and public-health significance
A pandemia de Covid-19 destacou a necessidade de se considerar esses eventos como fenômenos complexos, não-lineares e multipolares (clínica, econômica, social e política).
Limitations and evidence strength
Conclui-se que o enfrentamento requer aprendizado contínuo com a história e, no contexto brasileiro, o fortalecimento de um sistema de saúde público como o Sistema Único de Saúde (SUS), valorizando a Atenção Primária em Saúde, o Programa Nacional de Imunização e a integração de ações de vigilância em saúde entre os entes federativos.

新发传染病(猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯山脉):传播、环境生存和消毒的叙述性回顾

Emerging infectious diseases (Mpox, Nipah, and Andes): A narrative review of transmission, environmental survival, and disinfection
作者： David J. Weber, William A. Rutala, April Baller, Brooke E Brewer, Emily E. Sickbert-Bennett, E. Sickbert‐Bennett
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒是新出现的传染病,导致与医院 传播 相关的 暴发。方法:这篇叙述性综述总结了 猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒 流行病学、临床表现、诊断 和 治疗,重点是环境生存、对防腐剂和消毒剂的敏感性以及感染预防。结果:猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒都可以在环境中生存,并且能够间接感染 传播。所有这些病毒都有包膜,对常用的防腐剂和表面消毒剂敏感。结论:快速识别已知或疑似感染这些新出现病毒的患者,并遵守感染预防建议,将预防与医疗保健相关的 传播。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒是新出现的传染病,它们导致与院内 传播 相关的 暴发。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述总结了 猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒 流行病学、临床表现、诊断 和 治疗,重点是环境生存、对防腐剂和消毒剂的敏感性以及感染预防。
共识与结论
快速识别已知或疑似感染这些新兴病毒的患者,并遵守感染预防建议,将预防与医疗保健相关的 传播。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
提供的证据没有明确说明具体的研究或实践意义。

Emerging infectious diseases (Mpox, Nipah, and Andes): A narrative review of transmission, environmental survival, and disinfection

Authors: David J. Weber, William A. Rutala, April Baller, Brooke E Brewer, Emily E. Sickbert-Bennett, E. Sickbert‐Bennett
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus are emerging infectious diseases that cause outbreaks associated with nosocomial transmission. METHODS: This narrative review summarizes the Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus epidemiology, clinical manifestations, diagnosis, and treatment with a focus on environmental survival, susceptibility to antiseptics and disinfectants, and infection prevention. RESULTS: Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus all survive in the environment and are capable of indirect transmission. All these viruses are enveloped and susceptible to commonly used antiseptics and surface disinfectants. CONCLUSIONS: Rapid identification of patients with known or suspected infections of these emerging viruses coupled with adherence to infection prevention recommendations will prevent healthcare-associated transmission.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus are emerging infectious diseases that cause outbreaks associated with nosocomial transmission.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
Rapid identification of patients with known or suspected infections of these emerging viruses coupled with adherence to infection prevention recommendations will prevent healthcare-associated transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific research or practice implication.
